## `AGO-8522-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Vocational-CNC-Machining Master
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Vocational High School CNC / Robotics Practicum Trainer
- **Intro:** Vocational-CNC-Machining Master — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Vocational High School CNC / Robotics Practicum Trainer — Verify Machine Power-Off and LOTO Status: Air pressure must be 0 bar; main power switch must be in OFF position; LOTO lock must be applied with a unique key held by the operator.
